Cognition\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/c052b17f3fb86e49a5847a01d3d806e02770b85c-212x212.png\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/4c4b7add67179438039f9fb3f6e8958c88c79425-200x200.png\",\"quote\":\"The training recipe was precisely tailored to our product and use case. Applied Compute's approach to dataset, environment, and harness construction gave us confidence that the model would perform well in the production setting with real users.\",\"slug\":\"cognition\"},\"doordash\":{\"author\":\"Andy Fang\",\"authorRole\":\"Co-Founder, CTO at DoorDash\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/6fcf5d91219cc11d732bd73f457f0548cda73b9b-165x165.png\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/23ce3282d38862b5139c1b8ef14209e22d398d70-200x200.png\",\"quote\":\"AI has long been core to how DoorDash helps merchants succeed. By encoding our quality standards directly into model training, we scaled our internal expertise and raised the bar on menu accuracy on the platform.\",\"slug\":\"doordash\"},\"mercor\":{\"author\":\"Brendan Foody\",\"authorRole\":\"Co-Founder, Co-CEO at Mercor\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/6841156edba23de6b6cf5a6f06a282d254ec8323-1448x1448.png\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/c58a9d752ea0903f35ea543bbd384c22ff8aeffb-240x240.png\",\"quote\":\"Applied Compute achieving frontier capabilities on APEX Agents with just 2,000 tasks is incredible.\",\"slug\":\"mercor\"},\"bridge\":{\"author\":\"Zach Abrams\",\"authorRole\":\"Co-Founder, CEO at Bridge\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/2d73cd34cc804f3e67d9f62b98e0024c4c039183-200x200.jpg\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/86f20a55b884327995724a15a41d4ed5f620ffe1-800x800.jpg\",\"quote\":\"Our support team's judgment is the hardest thing to encode: subtle differences between failures, when to escalate, what's safe to surface. Applied Compute built an agent that learns that judgment from our SMEs in production, so our expertise compounds with every ticket instead of living in a few people's heads\",\"slug\":\"bridge\"},\"latch-bio\":{\"author\":\"Kenny Workman\",\"authorRole\":\"Co-Founder, CTO at Latch Bio\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/e4062b41121f70edc6c941a88bbda6f003898af0-1580x1136.png\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/5bf1f3c60b937ccbe2873697daff2dab1fa210f3-572x632.png\",\"quote\":\"Scientifically reliable agents require robust benchmarks. Applied Compute helped us better understand how well our benchmarks map to practical tasks, allowing us to further improve agents on biology.\",\"slug\":\"latch-bio\"},\"harvey-review-table\":{\"author\":\"Gabe Pereyra\",\"authorRole\":\"Co-Founder, President at Harvey\",\"logoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/d22ab8563d5542fa51381dea48f36df051fe6ed0-1024x1024.png\",\"photoSrc\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/dffea621f6bd81ecf6ae4d38022328480e576f0d-400x400.jpg\",\"quote\":\"Applied Compute's platform gives us the high-performance training infrastructure we need to run large-scale RL inside a custom legal-agent harness.
